HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B005","root_001645/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_01920c56b7dc121f426d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:6:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B005\"]],[\"101:6:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ağırlığın kıymet boyutu, kişiye yalnızca miktar değil korunmaya değer bir konum kazandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B005","root_000202/B007","root_001645/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0610186eacbf97490787","text":"The immediate `عِيشَةٍ رَّاضِيَةٍ` in 101:7 confirmed `ث ق ل B005` and `و ز ن B007`: heaviness carries worth because it yields a fitting condition. The exact light opposite in 101:8 stabilized the comparative mechanism of `و ز ن B003`. The unexpected mother noun in 101:9 then activated the exploratory pregnancy branch `ث ق ل B007`, making heaviness outcome-bearing rather than static; 101:11 shows how sharply the alternative outcome diverges.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_173f1d9ff73139c43afe","text":"4. **Exploratory generative heaviness.** `ث ق ل B007` is pregnancy becoming heavy. The heavy clause immediately bears `عِيشَةٍ` in 101:7, while the opposite sequence unexpectedly introduces `أُمُّهُ` in 101:9. These later life and mother terms activate a generative image in the fixed verb: weight carries an outcome until it comes forth. The packet does not grammatically identify scales with a pregnant body, so this is a material analogy rather than a lexical substitution. It changes the heavy balances from static measurement into outcome-bearing capacity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B004","root_001645/B001","root_001645/B002","root_001645/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1dc77f79b28ccb149366","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:6:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B004\"]],[\"101:6:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B001\",\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Çoğul tartılar, kişiyi tek bir ölçüye indirmeyen, karşılaştırmalı ve denetleyici bir adalet düzeni düşündürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001645/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_431a80df0eef7d54b392","text":"3. **The plural balances align a person with a standard.** `و ز ن B003` covers balancing or aligning two things. The paired `فَأَمَّا` and `وَأَمَّا` clauses align heavy and light cases, while their consequences align a satisfying life against abyss and fire. This branch gives `مَوَٰزِينُهُ` the role of relational comparators, not a heap of isolated instruments. I infer an unstated standard against which the person is aligned; the packet displays opposition but does not name that standard. The ayah consequently reads as placement within a structured comparison.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B006","root_001645/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_54074aa6c780ebcf84e6","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:6:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B006\"]],[\"101:6:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ağırlık, önceki savrulmayı durduran bir kararlılık ve yerleşiklik işlevi de görür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_74e00af0c45dcad9134a","text":"101:7 confirmed that heavy weight is protective and preservative, not merely quantitative. 101:8 sharpened the contrast, while 101:11 made the alternative containment fiery, increasing the sense that the scales decide which enclosure receives the person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_75a2f1ecae3cc06720a6","text":"2. **Weight as worth, not volume.** `ث ق ل B005` gives preciousness and high worth, while `و ز ن B007` gives standing or value that carries weight. The immediate result in 101:7 is `عِيشَةٍ رَّاضِيَةٍ`, in contrast to the light-scale sequence in 101:8-11. The branches assign qualitative value to the heaviness, and the outcome functions as evidence of that value. The arrow from valuable weight to fitting life is supplied by `فَهُوَ`, but the claim that worth rather than quantity explains it is abductive. The line thus reads as a verdict of substantial standing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7cee304ec24f949138b9","text":"1. **Assessed heaviness survives the loss of physical mass.** `ث ق ل B001` supplies heaviness against lightness, and `و ز ن B002` supplies the scale of justice and accounting of deeds. In 101:5 the mountains lose compact mass; in 101:6 and 101:8 balances nevertheless divide into heavy and light. The two branches make heaviness an assessed relation rather than remaining physical bulk. I infer that the scales register accountable content; the packet provides the justice branch but does not enumerate what is weighed. The ayah reads as a transfer of \"weight\" from unstable matter to durable evaluation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001","root_000202/B004","root_000202/B005","root_000202/B006","root_001645/B001","root_001645/B002","root_001645/B005","root_001645/B007"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_85e5b4f03d328e31e943","text":"1. Moral weight after cosmic de-weighting. Evidence: ث ق ل B001, heaviness against lightness; و ز ن B002, balance of justice and accounting; ج ب ل B001 with ن ف ش B001 in 101:5, solid mountains fluffed apart. Structural cue: the فَأَمَّا division follows the dissolution of mountains. Mechanism: once physical heaviness is destabilized, the scale introduces another register of weight that still counts. Abductive move: I infer contrastive sequencing: cosmic mass loses force so accountable weight can appear. Reading change: heavy scales read as moral gravity, not simply quantity. 2. Preciousness preserved. Evidence: ث ق ل B005, precious protected weight/high worth; و ز ن B007, standing or value that carries weight; ر ح م B001 at 101:0, mercy/beneficence; ع ي ش B001 in 101:7, good lived condition. Structural cue: heavy scales immediately lead to a satisfying life. Mechanism: the scale recognizes worth and protects it into the next state. Abductive move: I supply the protective consequence between measured value and pleasant living. Reading change: ثقلت means the person carries precious significance, not only a heavy balance-pan. 3. Calibration and justice. Evidence: و ز ن B001, measuring by weight or estimation; و ز ن B002, equity/justice/accounting; ث ق ل B004, standard weight and known measure. Structural cue: موازين is plural and paired later with خفت موازينه. Mechanism: multiple measures cross-check a person, converting dispersed life into determinate judgment. Abductive move: I infer a forensic accounting process from the plural scales. Reading change: the ayah reads as measured adjudication, not a single metaphor of heaviness. 4. Steadiness against swarm-instability. Evidence: و ز ن B005, steady judgment; ث ق ل B006, slowness/dragging heaviness; ن و س B001 and ف ر ش B005 in 101:4, swaying and fluttering lightness. Structural cue: the syntax moves from chaotic motion to conditional stability. Mechanism: weight arrests the earlier oscillation; it gives a person enough steadiness to resist the swarm-state. Abductive move: I infer that weight functions dynamically as stabilization. Reading change: \"heavy scales\" becomes the recovery of settledness after disorientation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B001","root_001645/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_97bbbc98b5a68b80a0a2","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:6:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B001\"]],[\"101:6:4\",\"root_001645\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kozmik kütle çözüldükten sonra da ağır kalan tartılar, belirleyici ağırlığın maddi değil hesap verilebilir bir değer olduğunu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000202/B007"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ab4d6ae02e0f32134b10","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"101:6:3\",\"root_000202\",[\"B007\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Gebelikte ağırlaşma boyutu, tartıların sonraki yaşam sonucunu içinde taşıyıp doğuran bir kapasite gibi okunmasına yol açar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
